# Card 241: full-objective soak of FED_C2M1 (senior order 2026-11-12). Every objective of the real play route is

# client replay by log line. Order is the user's real play (playtest-menu-20261107-172301): O01, O02, O04, O03, O05.

# recorded, not fixed: failsoft. Generated by gen_c241.py (junior scratchpad).
